Let's parse state of art: "Starting from a nonnegative step function f=(a_0,...,a_{n-1}) normalized so that sum_j a_j = sqrt(2n), set M=||f*f||_∞. Next compute g_0=(b_0,...,b_{n-1}) by solving a linear program, i.e. maximizing sum_j b_j subject to b_j≥0 and ||f*g_0||_∞≤M; as is standard, the optimum is attained at an extreme point determined by an active set of binding inequalities, here corresponding to important constraints where the convolution bound (f*g_0)(x)≤M is tight and limiting. Rescale g_0 to match the normalization, g = sqrt(2n)/sum b * g_0, update f←(1-t)f+t g. Repeating produces sequence with nonincreasing ||f*f||∞..."

This is essentially Frank-Wolfe for minimizing max convolution over simplex? Convolution constraint f*g0≤M. If f, g nonnegative, max includes central = ||f||^2? Hard top-k: get side indices, sort? Use argpartition for top_k. Need top positions full length. Implement:
```
def top_k_weights_side(conv, k):
   mask = np.ones(conv.size,bool); mask[center]=False
   cside = conv.copy(); cside[center]=-1
   idx = np.argpartition(cside, -k)[-k:]
   q=zeros; q[idx]=1/k; return q

def hard_top_weights(conv, center, side_mask, k):
   cside=conv[side_mask]
   k=min(k, cside.size)
   idx_global = np.flatnonzero(side_mask) # but pass array
   if k==cside.size: q uniform side
   else: part = np.argpartition(cside, -k)[-k:]
   idx = side_indices[part]
   q=np.zeros(conv.size); q[idx]=1/k
```

Candidate update:
```
def multiplicative_update(a, h, eta):
   denom = max(np.max(np.abs(h)), 1e-300)
   loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - eta * h / denom
   loga -= np.max(loga)
   out = np.exp(loga)
   out = clip(out,0,None); S=out.sum(); if S small return uniform? return out/S
```
Should we use h - mean? Since normalization removes adding constant; h/denom. If h all positive, subtract mean maybe to preserve? Multiplicative with h high reduces. Good.

def run_multiplicative(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag='', iters=2000):
  a=normalize_a(a); n=len(a)
  best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_conv(a)
  side_mask, side_indices = side masks
  beta = 100.0 # maybe
  eta=0.5
  hard_k = 20
  stagnant=0; max_stagn=20
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time>deadline: break
    conv = fast_self_conv(a)
    sc = norm_score_side(conv,n)
    # compute q soft
    q_soft, maxc = soft_weights(conv, center, side_mask, beta)
    # select weight candidates: maybe q_soft, q_top, q_var if beta change
    best_cand=None; best_cand_sc=sc
    for q in weight_list:
       h=corr_pair(a,q)
       # quick if maxh<=0 continue
       # choose etas depending eta: [eta*0.5, eta, eta*2]
       for et in etas:
          upd=exp_update(a,h,et)
          sc2=score_norm(fast_self_conv(upd),n) # sum 1
          # maybe also blend 0.5
          if sc2<best_cand_sc: ...
          blend=0.3*upd+0.7*a; scb=...
    if best_cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-12: update best, stagnant=0; maybe eta*=1.2
    else: stagnant++; eta*=0.8; beta*= maybe if stagnant%5==0 alternate beta*2 or /2; hard_k increase; if stagnant>max_stagn: diversify best_a? break to perturb
  return best_sc,best_a
```

Now LSE log-space. Need implement `lse_lgfun_grad(x, beta, n_pre?)` top-level. Since top-level cannot have closures, but can have parameters. `minimize` calls with x and args beta. It can access n via len(x). Good.

```
def lse_lgfun_grad(p, beta):
    p = np.asarray(p, float)
    n = p.size
    p = p - float(np.mean(p))  # center
    maxp = np.max(p)
    logsum = maxp + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(p-maxp)))
    a = np.exp(p - logsum)
    conv = fast_self_conv(a)
    center = n-1
    # side d = 2n * (conv with center -1)
    cmax_side = np.max(conv with center -inf)
    if cmax_side <=0: return large, zeros
    d = 2.0*n*conv
    d[center] = -np.inf
    dm = maxd = 2n*cmax_side
    z = beta*(d - maxd)
    z = z[~isinf]? Need compute side only.
    zside = beta*(2n*conv_side - maxd)
    zside = np.clip(zside, -50, 0)
    e = np.exp(zside)
    Z = e.sum(); if Z<=0: q uniform side; else q_side=e/Z
    L = maxd + np.log(Z)/beta  # objective LSE
    # q full zeros except side
    q = np.zeros(conv.size); q[side_indices] = q_side  # side_indices precomputed? Cannot pass? Could compute mask each call? n fixed maybe global? We can compute inside: center=n-1; side_indices = np.r_[0:center, center+1:2*n-1] maybe.
    h = corr_pair(a,q)
    grad = 2.0*n*h
    # project:
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad))
    gradp = grad - a*dot
    # replace nonfinite
    return float(L), gradp
```

def fw_minimize_quadratic(M, m, iters=500, eps=1e8):
    nvar=M.shape[0]
    q=np.full(nvar,1/nvar)
    for it in range(iters):
       grad = 2*(M+epsI? but eps included in M) @ q
       gmin_idx = np.argmin(grad)
       # linear min oracle for f(q)=0.5 q^T M q? If we use objective q^T M q, gradient 2Mq. Frank-Wolfe step: s=1/n? Actually min over simplex of gradient dot z = grad[min].
       # Line search: minimize f((1-t)q+t e_min) over t in [0,1]
       # f(t)=0.5 ((1-t)q + t e)^T M ...; We can do exact 1D line search by parabola since quadratic.
```
Better objective use 0.5 q^T M q to simplify. Gradient = M q. For f=0.5 q^T M q, min oracle e_j. Step with exact line search:
Let d = e_j - q. f(t)=0.5(q+td)^T M(q+td)=f0 + t (grad^T d) + 0.5 t^2 d^T M d. Since M PD, denominator positive. t* = - (grad^T d)/(d^T M d), clip [0,1]. grad^T d = grad_j - q^T M q. d^T M d = M[j,j] -2 M[j,:]@q + q^T M q. Compute. This is standard LW? Use. If t*<=0 break. Update.

   n=len(a); h=np.zeros(n,float)
   for k,w in zip(lags,weights):
      if w<=0: continue
      k=int(k); if k>=n: continue
      h[:n-k] += w*a[k:]
      h[k:] += w*a[:n-k]
   # q normalized sum1, but derivative of full score with both sides has factor? If one-sided weights sum1, h dimension. Good.

def lse_raw_fun_grad(x,beta):
  x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x,...),0,None)
  S=float(np.sum(x)); if S<=1e-8: return 1e10, zeros
  n=x.size; conv=fast_self_conv(x); center=n-1
  d=2.0*n*conv/(S*S)
  # mask center: set d[center]= -inf
  side_indices pre? create side = d.copy(); side[center]=-1e100? Need maxd among side. Could create cmax_side=np.max(conv[side_mask])*2n/S^2.
  maxd=...
  z=beta*(d-maxd); set center -inf; clip.
  e=np.exp(z); Z=sum; q=zeros; q[side_mask]=e/Z
  L=maxd+log(Z)/beta
  h=corr_pair(x,q)
  dot=float(np.dot(q,d)) # center zero
  grad=(2.0*n/S*S)*h - (2.0*dot/S)*x
  # clip/replace nonfinite
  return L, grad
```

`fast_self_conv(a, N=None)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
if n==0: return empty
M=2*n-1
if N is None: N=next_power_of_two(M)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
return np.nan_to_num(out,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
```
`corr_pair(a,w,N=None)`:
```
n=a.size; m=w.size; if n==0 or m==0 empty
M=n+m-1
if N is None: N=next_power_of_two(M)
A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
out=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
return np.nan_to_num(out[n-1:2*n-1],...)
```
Could pass N_corr. Since w length maybe `w.size` full side `2n-1`; N_corr=next_power_of_two(3n-2) if w full. If w is one-sided length n, N self. For soft weights use full. For raw LSE use full. Good.

```
def max_excluding(conv, idx):
    m=conv.size
    if m<=1: return 0.0
    left = np.max(conv[:idx]) if idx>0 else -1e300
    right = np.max(conv[idx+1:]) if idx+1<m else -1e300
    v=max(float(left),float(right)); return max(0.0,v)
```

```
def soft_full_weights(conv, side_indices, n, beta):
   c=conv[side_indices]
   if c.size==0: return zeros
   maxc=float(np.max(c)); base=2.0*n*maxc
   z=beta*2.0*n*(c-maxc)
   z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
   e=np.exp(z); Z=e.sum()
   q=np.zeros(conv.size,float)
   if Z<=0 or not finite:
       q[side_indices]=1.0/c.size
   else: q[side_indices]=e/Z
   return q, 2.0*n*maxc
```
If beta=0, z=0, uniform. Good.

`exp_update(a,h,eta,mode='mult')`:
```
h=np.nan_to_num(h,...)
hmax=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
if hmax<=1e-300: return a.copy()

```

`qp_weights_for_lags(a, conv, lags, iters=300)`:
Input `lags` positive 1..n-1. Need C0=conv[center], Cpos=conv[center+1:] (length n-1). Build Mvar.
```
def qp_weights(lags, conv, center, n):
    m=len(lags)
    if m==0: return None
    Cpos=conv[center+1:]
    C0=float(conv[center])
    M=np.zeros((m,m))
    t=np.zeros(m)
    for ia,k in enumerate(lags):
       k=int(k)
       t[ia]=2.0*Cpos[k-1]
       diag=2.0*C0
       if 2*k < n: diag += 2.0*Cpos[2*k-1]
       M[ia,ia]=diag
    for ia in range(m):
       k=int(lags[ia])
       for ib in range(ia+1,m):
          l=int(lags[ib])
          diff=abs(k-l)
          val=2.0*(Cpos[diff-1] if diff>0 else C0)
          if k+l < n: val += 2.0*Cpos[k+l-1]
          M[ia,ib]=val; M[ib,ia]=val
    Mvar = M - np.outer(t,t)/float(n)
    # symmetrize, add reg
    Mvar = 0.5*(Mvar+Mvar.T)
    scale=max(1e-300, float(np.max(np.abs(np.diag(Mvar)))))
    eps=1e-7*scale
    Mvar += eps*np.eye(m)
    # FW
    q=np.full(m,1.0/m)
    I=np.eye(m) maybe not.
    for _ in range(iters):
       grad = Mvar @ q  # gradient of 0.5 q^T M q
       j=int(np.argmin(grad))
       # d = e_j - q
       # current = grad @ q
       cur=float(grad @ q)
       num = cur - grad[j]
       # den = Mjj -2 M[j,:].@q + cur
       row=Mvar[j]
       den=float(Mvar[j,j] - 2.0*float(row @ q) + cur)
       if den <= 1e-300:
           # use largest step? maybe 1/m
       else:
           tstep = num/den
           if tstep <=0: break
           if tstep>1: tstep=1.0
       q = (1-tstep)*q + tstep*eeee? e_j.
       # If q nearly unit, break
       if tstep < 1e-8: break
    q=np.clip(q,0,None); q/=q.sum()
    return q
```
But note objective 0.5 q^T M q; line search `num = cur - grad[j]` because grad^T d = grad^T(e_j - q)=grad[j]-cur; t* = -(grad^T d)/den = (cur - grad[j])/den. Good.
Potential issue Mvar can have negative denom due numerical; add reg.
FW iters 200 enough. If m large 300, M@q 90k ops; 200=18M; okay.
Need avoid O(m^2) Python loops for 300: 45k loops ok. Use Python loops maybe 0.05s. Fine.

Then use `h_from_lags_weights(a,lags,q,N? loop)` to compute h. We can use loop. `h_from_lags_weights` top-level:
```
def h_from_lags_weights(a, lags, weights):
   n=len(a); h=np.zeros(n,float)
   for idx,k in enumerate(lags):
      w=weights[idx]
      if w<=1e-15: continue
      k=int(k)
      if k<=0 or k>=n: continue
      h[:n-k] += w*a[k:]
      h[k:] += w*a[:n-k]
   hmax=np.max(np.abs(h)); if hmax>0: maybe no normalize? If weights sum1. Return h
```
Then run candidate etas.

```
def select_active_lags(pos_vals,k,rng=None,random_frac=0.0,threshold_frac=0.2):
   L=pos_vals.size
   k=min(k,L)
   if k<=0: return empty
   top_part=np.argpartition(pos_vals, -k)[-k:]
   lags_list = [int(i+1) for i in top_part]
   if rng is not None and k < L:
       maxv=float(np.max(pos_vals))
       thresh=maxv*(1-random_frac)
       candidates=np.where(pos_vals>=thresh)[0]
       extra = min(len(lags_list), L-len(top_part))
       if candidates.size>0 and extra>0:
           chosen=rng.choice(candidates, size=min(extra,candidates.size), replace=False)
           for c in chosen: set add
   # ensure unique 
   arr=np.unique(np.array(lags_list))
   return arr
```

Now LSE raw:
```
def lse_raw_fun_grad(x, beta, N_self, N_corr, side_indices, n, center):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel(); x=np.nan_to_num(x,...); x=np.clip(x,0,None)
   n=x.size
   S=float(np.sum(x))
   if S < 1e-10:
       return 1e12, np.zeros_like(x)
   conv=fast_self_conv(x,N_self)
   d=2.0*n*conv/(S*S)
   d[center]=0.0
   # side values for max
   cside=d[side_indices]
   if cside.size==0: return 0.0, zeros
   maxd=float(np.max(cside))
   if maxd <= 0 or not finite:
       return 1e12, zeros
   z=beta*(d - maxd)
   z[center]=-1e300
   z=np.clip(z, -50, 0)
   e=np.exp(z)
   Z=float(e.sum())
   q=np.zeros_like(d)
   if Z<=0:
       q[side_indices]=1.0/len(side_indices)
   else:
       q[side_indices]=e/Z
   L=float(maxd + np.log(Z)/beta)
   h=corr_pair(x,q,N_corr)
   dot=float(np.dot(q,d))
   grad=(2.0*n/(S*S))*h - (2.0*dot/S)*x
   grad=np.nan_to_num(grad,...); grad=np.clip(grad,-1e8,1e8)
   return L, grad
```
But note `d` center set 0; `z` for side ok. Dot q with d uses center 0. Good.
`np.log(Z)` if Z>0. For large beta, Z at least 1 (max term exp0), logZ≥0. L≈maxd.
L-BFGS `ftol` on L ~1.5; good.

Potential issue `bounded` values: If x has zeros, gradient maybe negative causing below bound, handled. If x all small? S scale invariant, if values scaled to ~1e-12, S small? But bounds at 10; optimizer may scale. We can if S <1e-5, return value at normalized? To avoid flat, we can inside function normalize x? But LSE raw is scale invariant; if S underflows, no. We can if S<1e-5 rescale x? The function cannot modify input for line search, but can compute using x/S? For S very small, C/S^2 stable? If x ~1e-12, C~1e-24, S^2~1e-24, ratio okay but underflow if smaller. We can if S<1e-3, x=x/S (scale up) before conv to use normalized; but gradient w.r.t original x? If we rescale internally, gradient changes? Since objective invariant, gradient at scaled x should same (scale invariant). If we compute using y=x/S, grad_y; grad_x = grad_y/S? Actually f(x)=f(y), y=x/S. derivative df/dx = (1/S) df/dy. If line search in x, can return grad_x. But easier: if S too small, normalize x in computation and return L, grad = grad_y/S? But at very small scale, gradient tiny; L-BFGS may think stationary. Better avoid small S by bounds? Starting normalized S=1; optimizer may not scale down due radial flat. But if it does, we can just compute with normalized and return grad wrt x as `0`? No, direction matters. Could set if S<1e-3: y=x/S; return fun/grad_y (not scaled)?? Line search step sizes independent? Since f invariant, gradient should be zero along radial but not along shape; in x coordinates, shape gradient scales 1/S. If S small, gradient large maybe. We can compute y=x/S; grad_y; grad_x = grad_y / S (because x=S y? y=x/S, dy/dx=1/S; df/dx=df/dy*1/S). If S small, gradient large. Let's implement: if S <1e-3: x_used=x/S; grad computed with x_used,S_used=1; return L, grad / S (clip). But if S tiny, overflow. Could just scale x to sum 1 inside and return grad (not /S) because optimizer may scale back? Hmm. Simpler: In `lse_raw_fun_grad`, we can normalize x to sum 1 at start for computation, and return gradient w.r.t that normalized x? But optimize variable x not normalized; returning grad not consistent scale leads line search maybe still okay? To be safe, if S !=1, compute y=x/S, d=2n C_y (S=1), h_y, grad_y=2n h_y -2 dot y? Since S=1. df/dx = grad_y / S. Use that. So:
```
S=x.sum(); if S small return inf
y=x/S
conv=... y
d=2n*conv
maxd...
h=corr_pair(y,q)
grad_y=2n*h - 2*dot*y # since S=1, alpha=2n
grad=grad_y / S
```
This gives correct and stable. If S=1, same. Good. Use this.

```
def run_lse_raw(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, side_indices, n, center):
   best_a=normalize_a(a); best_sc, _=score_array(best_a)
   betas=[10,30,100,300,1000,3000,10000]
   for idx,b in enumerate(betas):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline-20: break
       x0=normalize_a(best_a).copy()
       # small random perturbation for high beta? maybe if idx>2 and rng? But function no rng? can pass.
       maxfun = 300 if b<2000 else 450
       # time-aware: rem=deadline-time; maxfun=min(maxfun, max(100,int(rem/0.05)))? If each fun 0.05, rem=300, maxfun 400. Use maxfun=min(maxfun, max(80,int(rem*3)))? Hard. Use fixed 350.
       opts={'maxiter':200,'maxfun':350,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':30}
       try:
          res=minimize(lse_raw_fun_grad, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(b,N_self,N_corr,side_indices,n,center), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
          y=clip(res.x)
       except: y=x0
       # evaluate several beta checkpoints? minimize returns final; but best may be before final. We can't know path. Could run with maxfun maybe final good. Or periodically? Not easy.
       sc,_=score_array(y)
       if sc<best_sc: best...
       # if final worse, maybe try a smaller maxfun result? We don't save intermediate. Could call minimize with maxfun half then full, use best of both.
```

Log LSE helper:
```
def lse_log_fun_grad(p,beta,N_self,N_corr,side_indices,n,center):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p-=p.mean()
   maxp=max(p); logsum=maxp+log(sum(exp(p-maxp))); a=exp(p-logsum)
   conv=fast_self_conv(a,N_self)
   d=2n*conv; d=center0; maxd...
   z=beta*(d-maxd); z[center]=-inf; e...
   q...
   L=maxd+logZ/beta
   h=corr_pair(a,q,N_corr)
   grad=2n*h
   dot=float(np.dot(a,grad))
   gradp=grad - a*dot
   return L, gradp
```

Soft weights full:
```
def softmax_weights(conv, beta):
   maxc = float(np.max(conv))
   if maxc <=0: uniform
   z=beta*(2*n*conv - 2n*maxc) = beta*norm_score*conv
   z=clip(z,-50,0)
   e=exp(z); Z=e.sum(); return e/Z, maxc
```
No mask. This will focus on all coefficients near max, including center and edges.

Top-k weights full: top k of conv.

Variance weights full: top m and excess above mean top.

Multiplicative update same.

`lse_raw_fun_grad(x,beta,...)`:
```
S=sum(x); if S<=tiny return inf
a=x/S
c=fast_self_conv(a)
maxc=max(c)
z=beta*(c-maxc) # c units
e=exp clipped; Z=sum
q=e/Z
L=2.0*n*(maxc + log(Z)/beta)  # approx score
h=corr_pair(a,q)
dot=float(np.dot(a,h))
grad = 4.0*n*(h - dot*a)
if S !=1: grad = grad / S
return L, grad

Let's design `score_array` returns clipped conv and score:
```
def score_array(a, N_self):
  a=normalize_a(a)
  c=fast_self_conv(a, N_self)
  c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
  score=2.0*len(a)*maxc
  return score, c
```

def softmax_weights(c,beta):
  maxc=float(np.max(c)); if maxc<=0: uniform
  z=beta*(c-maxc); z=np.clip(z,-50,0)
  e=np.exp(z); Z=sum
  if Z<=0: finite? uniform
  return e/Z, maxc
```

Top weights:
```
def top_weights(c,k):
  L=c.size; k=min(max(1,k),L)
  q=np.zeros(L)
  if k==L: q.fill(1/L)
  else:
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    q[idx]=1/k
  return q
```
Variance weights:
```
def excess_weights(c,top_m):
  L=c.size; k=min(max(1,top_m),L)
  idx=np.argpartition(c,-k)[-k:]
  vals=c[idx]; mean=np.mean(vals)
  excess=np.maximum(c-mean,0); s=excess
  if s<=1e-300:
     q=zeros; q[idx]=1/k
  else: q=excess/s
  # q sum1
  return q
```
Note if many above mean; okay.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-12
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  load parent; n, a
  N_self, N_corr
  best_a, best_sc = score_array(a)
  print
  # Chunk 1: multiplicative short (10%)
  d1=t0+0.10*budget_s
  sc,a=run_multiplicative(a,d1, N_self,N_corr, verbose=True, tag='m1', max_iter=500)
  update best
  # Chunk 2: LSE raw (40%)
  d2=t0+0.50*budget_s
  sc,a=run_lse_raw(a,d2,N_self,N_corr, ...)
  update
  # Chunk 3: multiplicative medium (15%)
  d3=t0+0.65*budget_s
  ...
  # Chunk 4: LSE high (15%)
  d4=t0+0.80*budget_s
  ...
  # Chunk 5: restarts (10%)
  d5=deadline-10
  for p in perturbations (3-5): run_multiplicative short
  # final short multiplicative (rest)
  return best
```

```
def run_lse_raw(a, deadline, N_self,N_corr,n,verbose,tag):
  best_a=normalize_a(a); best_sc=score_array(best_a)
  bounds=[(0.0,10.0)]*n
  betas=[2000,10000,50000,200000,1000000]
  for beta in betas:
    if time> deadline-20: break
    x=normalize_a(best_a).copy()
    # if beta large, maybe fewer runs; maxfuns=[150,300,600]
    for mf in [150,300,600]:
       if time> deadline-20: break
       rem=deadline-time
       # adjust mf: if rem < estimated, reduce. Use time per fun estimate dynamic? Could track elapsedfun.
       opts = {'maxiter': max(20,mf//2), 'maxfun': mf, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':30}
       try res=minimize(...)
       except: continue
       y=clip(res.x)
       sc=score_array(y)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=normalize_a(y)
       # else maybe continue from y if not too worse? Keep best to avoid degradation.
       if verbose and improvement: print
    # if no improvement at this beta, still next beta? yes.
  return best_sc,best_a
```

Pseudo code for `run_multiplicative`:

```
def run_multiplicative(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, verbose=False, tag=''):
    a=normalize_a(a); n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc,c_best=score_conv_norm? 
    c=fast_self_conv(a,N_self); c=max; best_sc=2n max
    beta=5000.0 # c units

def h_from_weights(a,q,threshold=1e-12,N_corr=None):
   nz=np.nonzero(q>threshold)[0]
   if nz.size==0: return corr_pair
   if nz.size <= 200:
      h=np.zeros(n)
      for r in nz:
         r=int(r); w=q[r]
         if r<0 or r>=2*n-1: continue
         # h_i += w*a_{r-i} for i=max(0,r-n+1) to min(r,n-1), j=r-i
         i_start=max(0, r-(n-1)); i_end=min(n-1, r)
         j_start=r - i_end; j_end=r - i_start
         h[i_start:i_end+1] += w * a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]
      return h
   else: return corr_pair(a,q,N_corr)
```

`h_from_weights` for sparse loop: We need q full length L. For each r, update slice. Complexity. Implementation:
```
def h_from_weights(a, q, N_corr=None, sparse_limit=300):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    q=np.asarray(q,float)
    if q.size != L: # if wrong, dense fail
    nz=np.flatnonzero(q > 1e-16)
    if nz.size == 0:
       return np.zeros(n)
    if nz.size <= sparse_limit:
       h=np.zeros(n,float)
       for rr in nz:
          r=int(rr); w=float(q[r]);
          if w==0: continue
          if r < 0 or r >= L: continue
          i_start = max(0, r - (n-1))
          i_end = min(n-1, r)
          if i_start > i_end: continue
          j_end = r - i_start
          j_start = r - i_end
          # j from j_start to j_end as i from i_start to i_end? For i=i_start, j=r-i_start=j_end; for i=i_end, j=j_start. Need slice reversed from j_end down to j_start.
          segment = a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]
          h[i_start:i_end+1] += w * segment
       return h
    else:
       if N_corr is None: return corr_pair(a,q)
       return corr_pair(a,q,N_corr)
```
Check mapping with simple n=3 q r=3 (center? c3=2a1a2), h=∑ q a_{3-i}. r=3: i_start=max(0,3-2)=1, i_end=min(2,3)=2. i=1 ->j=2; i=2 ->j=1. segment a[j_start=1:j_end=2+1=3][::-1] = [a2,a1], h[1]+=a2,h[2]+=a1. Expected derivative for coefficient c3=2a1a2: wrt a1=2a2? h one factor =a2 for i1; a1 for i2. good.

Dense `corr_pair` returns ∑ a_j q_{i+j}, same mapping? For q r, contributes a_j when i+j=r -> i=r-j. For fixed r, h_i += a_{r-i}. Yes. Our sparse same. Good.

Now, top weights: If q sparse, `h_from_weights` loop. In `corr_pair` dense, uses reversed a. Good.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def next_power_of_two(m):
   m=int(max(1,m)); return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def clean_array(a, maxv=1000.0):
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=maxv, neginf=0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,maxv)
   return arr

def normalize_a(a):
   arr=clean_array(a)
   S=arr.sum()
   if S<1e-300:
       if arr.size==0: return np.ones(1)/1
       return np.ones(arr.size)/arr.size
   return arr/S
```
If arr empty maybe fallback length 1? propose ensures n.

```
def run_multiplicative(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, verbose=False, tag=''):
   a=normalize_a(a); n=len(a)
   conv=fast_self_conv(a,N_self); conv=np.maximum(conv,0)
   best_sc=score_from_conv(normconv,n)
   best_a=a.copy()
   beta=100.0
   eta=0.5
   top_k=8
   var_k=30
   stag=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
      it +=1
      if it > 20000: break
      conv=fast_self_conv(a,N_self); conv=np.maximum(conv,0)
      sc=score_from_conv(conv,n)
      if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
      # build weights
      q_soft,_=softmax_weights(conv,n,beta)
      q_top=top_weights(conv,top_k)
      weights = [('soft',q_soft), ('top',q_top)]
      if stag > 5:
          q_var=excess_weights(conv, var_k); weights.append(('var',q_var))
      best_local_sc=sc; best_local_a=a.copy(); best_eta=eta
      for name,q in weights:
         h=h_from_weights(a,q,N_corr)
         hmax=np.max(np.abs(np.nan_to_num(h)))
         if hmax <=1e-300: continue
         # mult
         for et in (eta*0.5, eta, eta*2.0):
             upd=exp_update(a,h,et)
             sc2=score_normalized(upd,N_self)
             if sc2<best_local_sc:
                best_local_sc=sc2; best_local_a=upd; best_eta=et
         # if improved baseline but not best, try blend
         if best_local_sc < sc - 1e-13:
             bl=blend_normalize(a,best_local_a,0.5)
             scb=score_normalized(bl,N_self)
             if scb<best_local_sc:
                best_local_sc=scb; best_local_a=bl
         # if still no improvement for this q, try Euclidean maybe only one q to save
         if best_local_sc >= sc - 1e-13 and name != 'soft':
             for et in (0.2*eta, 0.8*eta, 2.0*eta):
                upd=euclid_update(a,h,et)
                sc2=score_normalized(upd,N_self)
                if sc2<best_local_sc: ...
      if best_local_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
         a=best_local_a; best_sc=best_local_sc; stag=0; eta=min(3.0, best_eta*1.1)
         if verbose and it%20==0: print
      else:
         stag +=1
         eta = max(0.05, eta*0.9)
         if stag % 5 == 0:
            if beta < 50000: beta *=2.0
            else: beta = max(50.0, beta*0.5)
         top_k = min(300, top_k + 2)
         var_k = min(300, var_k + 3)
         if stag > 300: break
   return float(best_sc), best_a.copy()
```

`lse_raw_fun_grad` as above. It uses `h_from_weights` with q; q may dense. It returns gradient. Need ensure finite if beta large. Z could be 1, log 0.

```
def lse_raw_fun_grad(x,beta,N_self,N_corr,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
   x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,10.0)
   S=float(np.sum(x))
   if S < 1e-8:
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
   a=x/S
   c=fast_self_conv(a,N_self)
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   d=2.0*float(n)*c
   maxd=float(np.max(d))
   if maxd <=0 or not finite:
       return 1e100, zeros
   z=beta*(d-maxd)
   z=np.clip(z,-50.0,0.0)
   e=np.exp(z); Z=float(e.sum())
   q=np.zeros_like(d)
   if Z <=0: q.fill(1/d.size)
   else: q=e/Z
   L=float(maxd + np.log(Z)/float(beta))
   h=h_from_weights(a,q,N_corr)
   h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   dot=float(np.dot(a,h))
   grad=4.0*float(n)*(h - dot*a)
   grad=grad/S
   grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   grad=np.clip(grad,-1e6,1e6)
   return L, grad
```
Note `betas` score units. If beta=50, z spread. Good.

One thing: LSE value `L = maxd + log(Z)/β`. If beta small 50, logZ/β≈0.18, L≈1.68. Gradient weights softmax. Good.

Potential L-BFGS line search: If value L has constant offset maxd, gradient matches. Good.

`run_lse_raw`:
```
def run_lse_raw(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, n, verbose=False, tag=''):
   best_a=normalize_a(a); best_sc=score_normalized(best_a,N_self)
   bounds=[(0.0,10.0)]*n
   betas=[50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
   last_time=time.monotonic(); per_fun=0.05
   for beta in betas:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15: break
      x=best_a.copy()
      improved_beta=False
      # maxfun plan depends on beta maybe
      for mf in [150,400,1000,2500]:
          rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
          if rem < 15: break
          allowed = int(max(50, rem*0.85 / max(1e-3, per_fun)))
          use_mf = min(mf, allowed)
          if use_mf < 50: break
          opts={'maxiter': max(20, use_mf//3), 'maxfun': use_mf, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':25}
          t_call=time.monotonic()
          try:
             res=minimize(lse_raw_fun_grad, x, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,N_self,N_corr,n), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
             y=np.asarray(res.x,float)
          except Exception:
             y=None
          elapsed=max(1e-6, time.monotonic()-t_call)
          # per fun estimate use use_mf if res not None else 1
          per_fun=max(1e-3, min(1.0, elapsed / float(use_mf)))
          if y is None: break
          y=np.nan_to_num(y,...); y=np.clip(y,0,10)
          sc=score_normalized(y,N_self)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best_sc=sc; best_a=normalize_a(y); x=best_a.copy(); improved_beta=True
              if verbose: print...
          else:
              # If result finite but not better, maybe keep it if score close and beta high? use best to avoid drift.
              x=best_a.copy()
          if not improved_beta and use_mf >= 400:
              # skip remaining for this beta? maybe if high beta only?
              # Continue to next beta
              break
   return best_sc,best_a
```

Details for `resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
   a=clean_array(a); m=a.size
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if m==n: return a
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   return normalize_a(np.interp(x_new,x_old,a))
```

For each q, after evaluating candidates, if `best_local_sc < best_sc` but not improved for previous q? best_local updated across q. If after first q improves, second q might use baseline `sc` (old) or `best_local_sc`? We set `best_local_sc=sc` initially and update; for each q, we compare candidates to `best_local_sc`, so it can build on previous q's best even if not updated a? We do not update a inside iteration, only `best_local_a`; candidates for second q are generated from *original a*, not from best_local_a unless we update. That's okay; we evaluate alternative directions from same a and choose best. If a candidate from first q is best, second q candidates from original a might be worse; final best is best. We don't combine. Good.

But if `best_local_sc` improved by first q, for second q Euclidean only if `best_local_sc >= sc - 1e-13` (which false), so skip extra. Good.

If no improvement, after iteration we keep a unchanged. This greedy may miss sequential small steps. LSE handles.

Could update a to best_local if it improves even if not best_sc? In code if `best_local_sc < best_sc`, best_sc should be current sc (unless score improved earlier). So any improvement triggers. Good.

Now, in `run_multiplicative`, after improvement, `a=best_local_a`. But if candidate was generated from old a with euclidean, normalized. Good.

Now, in `run_lse_raw`, if result y has S not 1, `score_normalized` normalizes. For next x, use `best_a` normalized if improved; if not, x=best_a. If y not improved but maybe on path, we reset to best; okay.

Potential issue: `score_normalized` expects a normalized but if input y from L-BFGS has S=100, it normalizes inside (we should). Let's implement `score_array(a,N_self)` always normalize; use for both. Candidate updates already normalized but safe.

def score_normalized(a,N_self):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
using exact FFT convolution scoring, active-set multiplicative gradients,
sharp LSE quasiconvex refinements (raw and log parameters), and perturb
restarts.  The implementation is tuned for the plateau near the 1.50 target.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def clean_array(a, maxv=1000.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=float(maxv), neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(arr, 0.0, float(maxv))


def normalize_a(a):
    arr = clean_array(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = clean_array(a)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_a(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return normalize_a(np.interp(x_new, x_old, a))


def fast_self_conv(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = next_power_of_two(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    total = n + m - 1
    if N is None:
        N = next_power_of_two(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:total]
    return np.nan_to_num(full[n - 1:2 * n - 1], nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def h_from_weights(a, q, N_corr=None, sparse_limit=320):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if q.size != L:
        return corr_pair(a, q, N_corr)
    nz = np.flatnonzero(q > 1e-16)
    if nz.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if nz.size <= sparse_limit:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for rr in nz:
            r = int(rr)
            w = float(q[r])
            if w == 0.0 or r < 0 or r >= L:
                continue
            i_start = max(0, r - (n - 1))
            i_end = min(n - 1, r)
            if i_start > i_end:
                continue
            j_end = r - i_start
            j_start = r - i_end
            h[i_start:i_end + 1] += w * a[j_start:j_end + 1][::-1]
        return np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return corr_pair(a, q, N_corr)


def score_normalized(a, N_self):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    n = int(arr.size)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if n == 0 or s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    arr = arr / s
    c = fast_self_conv(arr, N_self)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 2.0
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)


def softmax_weights(c, n, beta):
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    d = 2.0 * float(n) * c
    md = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(md) or md <= 0.0:
        q = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
        return q, 0.0
    z = float(beta) * (d - md)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        q = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = e / Z
    return q, md


def top_weights(c, k):
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, L)))
    q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if k == L:
        q.fill(1.0 / L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        q[idx] = 1.0 / k
    return q


def excess_weights(c, top_m):
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(top_m, L)))
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    vals = c[idx]
    mean_v = float(np.mean(vals))
    excess = np.maximum(c - mean_v, 0.0)
    s = float(excess.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        q[idx] = 1.0 / k
        return q
    q = excess / s
    return q


def exp_update(a, h, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    hm = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
    if hm <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - float(eta) * h / hm
    loga -= float(np.max(loga))
    out = np.exp(loga)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return out / s


def projected_log_update(a, h, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.size == 0 or h.size != a.size:
        return a.copy()
    dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
    direction = a * (h - dot * a)
    dm = float(np.max(np.abs(direction))) if direction.size else 0.0
    if dm <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - float(eta) * direction / dm
    loga -= float(np.max(loga))
    out = np.exp(loga)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return out / s


def euclid_update(a, h, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.size == 0 or h.size != a.size:
        return a.copy()
    dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
    d = h - dot * a
    dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if dn <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    out = a - float(eta) * d
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return out / s


def blend_normalize(a, b, t):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or a.size != b.size:
        return a.copy()
    out = (1.0 - float(t)) * a + float(t) * b
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return out / s


def run_multiplicative(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, verbose=False, tag=""):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(best_a, N_self)

    beta = 120.0
    eta = 0.6
    top_k = 8
    var_k = 30
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0 and it < 20000:
        it += 1
        c = fast_self_conv(a, N_self)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        q_soft, _ = softmax_weights(c, n, beta)
        q_top = top_weights(c, top_k)
        choices = [("soft", q_soft), ("top", q_top)]
        if stagnant > 4:
            q_var = excess_weights(c, var_k)
            choices.append(("var", q_var))

        local_best_sc = sc
        local_best_a = a.copy()
        local_best_eta = eta

        for _, q in choices:
            h = h_from_weights(a, q, N_corr)
            hm = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
            if hm <= 1e-300:
                continue

            cands = (
                exp_update(a, h, eta),
                projected_log_update(a, h, eta),
                euclid_update(a, h, 0.5 * eta),
            )
            for upd in cands:
                s2 = score_normalized(upd, N_self)
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < local_best_sc:
                    local_best_sc = s2
                    local_best_a = upd

            if local_best_sc < sc - 1e-13:
                upd2 = exp_update(a, h, 2.0 * eta)
                s3 = score_normalized(upd2, N_self)
                if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < local_best_sc:
                    local_best_sc = s3
                    local_best_a = upd2

                bl = blend_normalize(a, local_best_a, 0.5)
                s4 = score_normalized(bl, N_self)
                if np.isfinite(s4) and s4 < local_best_sc:
                    local_best_sc = s4
                    local_best_a = bl

        if np.isfinite(local_best_sc) and local_best_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            a = normalize_a(local_best_a)
            best_sc = local_best_sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            stagnant = 0
            eta = min(3.0, max(0.08, local_best_eta * 1.05))
            if verbose and it % 25 == 0:
                print("  [{}] it={} score={:.10f} beta={:.1f} eta={:.3f}".format(
                    tag, it, best_sc, beta, eta))
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.88)
            if stagnant % 5 == 0:
                if beta < 80000.0:
                    beta *= 2.0
                else:
                    beta = max(60.0, beta * 0.5)
            top_k = min(350, top_k + 2)
            var_k = min(350, var_k + 4)
            if stagnant > 350:
                break

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def lse_raw_fun_grad(x, beta, N_self, N_corr, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 10.0)
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    if S < 1e-8:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)

    a = x / S
    c = fast_self_conv(a, N_self)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    d = 2.0 * float(n) * c
    md = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if md <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(md):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)

    z = float(beta) * (d - md)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        q = np.full(d.size, 1.0 / d.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = e / Z

    L = float(md + np.log(Z) / float(beta))
    h = h_from_weights(a, q, N_corr)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * (h - dot * a) / S
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e7, 1e7)
    return L, grad


def lse_log_fun_grad(p, beta, N_self, N_corr, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=500.0, neginf=-500.0)
    p = np.clip(p, -500.0, 500.0)
    if p.size:
        p = p - float(np.mean(p))

    mp = float(np.max(p))
    a = np.exp(p - mp)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p)
    a = a / S

    c = fast_self_conv(a, N_self)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    d = 2.0 * float(n) * c
    md = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if md <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(md):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p)

    z = float(beta) * (d - md)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        q = np.full(d.size, 1.0 / d.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = e / Z

    L = float(md + np.log(Z) / float(beta))
    h = h_from_weights(a, q, N_corr)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
    g = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    dotg = float(np.dot(a, g))
    grad = a * (g - dotg)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e7, 1e7)
    return L, grad


def run_lse_raw(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, n, verbose=False, tag=""):
    best_a = normalize_a(a)
    best_sc = score_normalized(best_a, N_self)
    bounds = [(0.0, 10.0)] * int(n)

    plans = [
        (60.0, (120, 350)),
        (250.0, (150, 500, 1200)),
        (1000.0, (200, 600, 1600)),
        (5000.0, (200, 700, 1800, 3500)),
        (20000.0, (150, 500, 1500, 3500)),
        (100000.0, (120, 400, 1200)),
    ]

    per_fun = 0.045
    for beta, maxfuns in plans:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 18.0:
            break
        x = best_a.copy()
        improved_beta = False
        for mf in maxfuns:
            rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 18.0:
                break
            allowed = int(max(60, rem * 0.85 / max(1e-4, per_fun)))
            use_mf = int(min(mf, allowed))
            if use_mf < 60:
                break

            opts = {
                "maxiter": max(25, use_mf // 3),
                "maxfun": use_mf,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 30,
                "maxcor": 20,
            }
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    lse_raw_fun_grad,
                    x,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    args=(beta, N_self, N_corr, n),
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=opts,
                )
                y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                nfev = int(getattr(res, "nfev", use_mf) or use_mf)
            except Exception:
                y = None
                nfev = max(1, use_mf)
            elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)
            per_fun = max(1e-4, min(1.5, elapsed / max(1, nfev)))

            if y is None:
                break
            y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 10.0)
            sc = score_normalized(y, N_self)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_a(y)
                x = best_a.copy()
                improved_beta = True
                if verbose:
                    print("  LSE raw {} beta={:.0f} mf={} score={:.10f}".format(tag, beta, use_mf, best_sc))
            else:
                x = best_a.copy()

            if not improved_beta and use_mf >= 600:
                break

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def run_lse_log(a, deadline, N_self, N_corr, n, verbose=False, tag=""):
    best_a = normalize_a(a)
    best_sc = score_normalized(best_a, N_self)

    plans = [
        (250.0, (120, 350)),
        (1500.0, (150, 500, 1200)),
        (8000.0, (200, 700, 1600)),
        (30000.0, (150, 500, 1500)),
    ]

    per_fun = 0.05
    for beta, maxfuns in plans:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 18.0:
            break
        x0 = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
        x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))
        x = x0.copy()
        improved_beta = False
        for mf in maxfuns:
            rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 18.0:
                break
            allowed = int(max(60, rem * 0.85 / max(1e-4, per_fun)))
            use_mf = int(min(mf, allowed))
            if use_mf < 60:
                break

            opts = {
                "maxiter": max(25, use_mf // 3),
                "maxfun": use_mf,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 30,
                "maxcor": 20,
            }
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    lse_log_fun_grad,
                    x,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    args=(beta, N_self, N_corr, n),
                    options=opts,
                )
                y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=500.0, neginf=-500.0)
                y = y - float(np.mean(y))
                yy = np.exp(y - float(np.max(y)))
                yy = yy / float(yy.sum())
                sc = score_normalized(yy, N_self)
                nfev = int(getattr(res, "nfev", use_mf) or use_mf)
            except Exception:
                yy = None
                sc = float("inf")
                nfev = max(1, use_mf)
            elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)
            per_fun = max(1e-4, min(1.5, elapsed / max(1, nfev)))

            if yy is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_a(yy)
                improved_beta = True
                if verbose:
                    print("  LSE log {} beta={:.0f} mf={} score={:.10f}".format(tag, beta, use_mf, best_sc))

            if not improved_beta and use_mf >= 600:
                break

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def perturb(a, rng, strength=0.12):
    p = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    strength = float(max(1e-4, min(1.0, strength)))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 9))
    if mode == 0:
        q = p * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (np.ones(n) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = p[::-1]
    elif mode == 3:
        k = min(31, max(5, n // 500))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern /= kern.sum()
        noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, method="same")
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        q = p * np.exp(0.35 * strength * noise)
    elif mode == 4:
        nb = int(rng.integers(4, 24))
        blocks = 1.0 + 0.35 * strength * (2.0 * rng.random(nb) - 1.0)
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        arr = np.repeat(blocks, per)
        if arr.size < n:
            arr = np.concatenate([arr, np.full(n - arr.size, blocks[-1], dtype=np.float64)])
        q = p * arr[:n]
    elif mode == 5:
        k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, n // 200)))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        k = min(k, n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1)
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern /= kern.sum()
        q = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")
    elif mode == 6:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        q = p * (1.0 - 0.5 * strength * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    elif mode == 7:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        q = p * (1.0 + 0.5 * strength * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    else:
        q = p * (0.85 + 0.3 * rng.random(n))
    return normalize_a(np.clip(q, 0.0, None))


def append_init(inits, arr, cap):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    inits.append(normalize_a(arr))


def generate_inits(n, rng, cap=28):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    inits = []

    append_init(inits, np.ones(n), cap)
    for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2):
        append_init(inits, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2, cap)
        append_init(inits, np.maximum(1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2, 1e-4), cap)

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
            append_init(inits, 1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x), cap)
            append_init(inits, 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + 0.7), cap)

    for rate in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        append_init(inits, np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + 0.02, cap)
        append_init(inits, np.exp(-rate * np.abs(x - 0.5)) + 0.02, cap)
        append_init(inits, np.exp(-rate * x) + 0.02, cap)

    for den in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
        append_init(inits, rng.random(n) + den * rng.random(n), cap)

    N = next_power_of_two(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(N) * N
    for _ in range(4):
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.5)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, N)[:n]
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        append_init(inits, 1.0 + 0.8 * noise, cap)

    return inits


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + total - 14.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = clean_array(h)
            if p.size >= 256 and float(np.sum(p)) > 0.01:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n < 1024:
            a = resample_sequence(parent, 10240)
            n = a.size
        elif n > 24000:
            a = resample_sequence(parent, 16384)
            n = a.size
        else:
            a = normalize_a(parent)
    else:
        n = 10240
        inits = generate_inits(n, rng)
        best_init = inits[0]
        best_init_sc = score_normalized(inits[0], next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1))
        N_tmp = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
        for init in inits[1:]:
            s = score_normalized(init, N_tmp)
            if s < best_init_sc:
                best_init_sc = s
                best_init = init
        a = best_init
        print("No parent. Best init score={:.10f}".format(best_init_sc))

    n = int(a.size)
    N_self = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    N_corr = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)

    best_sc = score_normalized(a, N_self)
    best_a = normalize_a(a)
    print("Start n={} score={:.10f}".format(n, best_sc))

    d1 = t_start + 0.12 * total
    sc, aa = run_multiplicative(best_a, d1, N_self, N_corr, True, "m1")
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc, best_a = sc, normalize_a(aa)
        print("After m1: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    d2 = t_start + 0.55 * total
    sc, aa = run_lse_raw(best_a, d2, N_self, N_corr, n, True, "lse1")
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc, best_a = sc, normalize_a(aa)
        print("After LSE raw 1: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    d3 = t_start + 0.68 * total
    sc, aa = run_multiplicative(best_a, d3, N_self, N_corr, True, "m2")
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc, best_a = sc, normalize_a(aa)
        print("After m2: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    d4 = t_start + 0.85 * total
    sc, aa = run_lse_log(best_a, d4, N_self, N_corr, n, True, "lse2")
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc, best_a = sc, normalize_a(aa)
        print("After LSE log: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    restarts = 0
    max_restarts = 6
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0 and restarts < max_restarts:
        restarts += 1
        rem = float(deadline - 35.0 - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 55.0:
            break
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.035, 0.22))
        p = perturb(best_a, rng, strength)
        chunk = min(deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + rem * 0.70)
        sc, aa = run_multiplicative(p, chunk, N_self, N_corr, False, "r{}".format(restarts))
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = sc, normalize_a(aa)
            print("Restart {}: {:.10f}".format(restarts, best_sc))

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        sc, aa = run_multiplicative(best_a, deadline - 6.0, N_self, N_corr, False, "final")
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = sc, normalize_a(aa)

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 1024

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    print("DONE internal={:.10f} verified={} n={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start))
    return out
```
